# Experiment Journal

Append-only. Each run writes one JSONL wire trace + responses beside it.
Computed results (persisted for reuse/re-experiment comparison) live in
`results/` as timestamped JSON.

Narrative entries: `ENTRY-2026-09-22-001.md`, `ENTRY-2026-09-22-002.md`, `ENTRY-2026-09-24-003.md`

## Analyzed set (2026-09-24, Entry 005)

Round-3 rebuild. The analyzed set is EXACTLY:

- **Top level (`journal/*.jsonl`)**: the 22 traces from batches
  `20260924T233922` (profiles: pfs/mc/rc) and `20260924T233925`
  (ablations: 3 bases + 16 single-flip). Wire shape: documented tool-use
  blocks (`tool_use` / `tool_result`); records are envelopes
  `{"_record_meta": {...}, "request": <pure provider-visible request>}`.
- **`journal/sweep-traces/`**: the 177 traces referenced by sweep batch
  `20260924T234023` (56 conditions: 6 schedules x 3 lengths x 3 thresholds +
  features + repo2 + 9 mc_nowarm composition controls).

Everything else is under `journal/superseded-r2/` (Round-2-and-earlier wire
shapes and simulator semantics, plus the buggy interim Round-3 batches) and
is OUTSIDE the analyzed set. All analyzed traces + responses + the three
batch-result files are SHA-256-checksummed in `journal/MANIFEST.json`
(round tag `r3`; regenerate: `python3 experiments/make_manifest.py r3`).
`latest_replay_table.json` and `latest_sensitivity.json` cover exactly the
22 top-level traces (asserted by the verifier).

Headline (revision 4, verifier 423/423): churn-conditional ranking —
PFS first in 47/47 churn-active conditions under both cache models; at true
zero churn the ranking dissolves into measurement composition (exact
turn-only ties without compaction; MC's aggregate surplus = warmer
self-hits; the compact-mid residual = warmer clock, not compaction shape).
See `ENTRY-2026-09-24-005.md` and `paper/response_to_reviewers.md`.
